Implemented composite loss $L = L_{LM} + \lambda_c L_{\text{cal}} + \lambda_e L_{\text{evidence}} + \lambda_s L_{\text{safety}}$, incorporating an asymmetric 10x penalty on false acceptances ($c \ge 0.80, y = 0 \implies P_{\text{overconf}} = c^2 \times 10.0$) that penalizes confident errors $>46\times$ more heavily than uncertain errors.
